+def _resolve_base_reg_vol_normalizer(
+    df_local: pd.DataFrame,
+) -> tuple[np.ndarray, str]:
+    if "realized_volatility_24h" in df_local.columns:
+        vol = np.asarray(df_local["realized_volatility_24h"].values, dtype=np.float32)
+        source = "realized_volatility_24h"
+    elif "__barrier_pct__" in df_local.columns:
+        vol = np.asarray(df_local["__barrier_pct__"].values, dtype=np.float32)
+        source = "__barrier_pct__"
+    else:
+        vol = np.full(len(df_local), 1.0, dtype=np.float32)
+        source = "unit_fallback"
+    vol = np.where(np.isfinite(vol), np.abs(vol), np.nan).astype(np.float32, copy=False)
+    vol = np.clip(vol, 1e-6, None)
+    return vol, source
+

+def _build_base_regression_target(
+    df_local: pd.DataFrame,
+    *,
+    side: str,
+    y_ret: np.ndarray | None = None,
+) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    from .lgbm_based_mask_generation import (
+        _apply_linear_target_residualizer,
+        _fit_linear_target_residualizer,
+    )
+
+    if y_ret is None:
+        if "__y_ret__" not in df_local.columns:
+            raise KeyError(
+                "Base reg target requires __y_ret__ when y_ret is not provided"
+            )
+        y_ret_arr = np.asarray(df_local["__y_ret__"].values, dtype=np.float32)
+    else:
+        y_ret_arr = np.asarray(y_ret, dtype=np.float32)
+    if len(y_ret_arr) != len(df_local):
+        raise ValueError("Base reg target: df/y_ret length mismatch")
+
+    side_sign = np.float32(-1.0 if str(side).lower() == "short" else 1.0)
+    vol_scale, vol_source = _resolve_base_reg_vol_normalizer(df_local)
+    z = (side_sign * y_ret_arr / vol_scale).astype(np.float32, copy=False)
+
+    nuisance_arrays: dict[str, np.ndarray] = {}
+    nuisance_resolution: dict[str, str] = {}
+    for feat_name in TRAINING_RESIDUALIZATION_FEATURE_KEYS:
+        if feat_name in df_local.columns:
+            nuisance_arrays[feat_name] = np.asarray(
+                df_local[feat_name].values, dtype=np.float32
+            )
+            nuisance_resolution[feat_name] = feat_name
+
+    residualized = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float32).copy()
+    residualizer = None
+    residualization_status = "identity"
+    if nuisance_arrays:
+        try:
+            residualizer = _fit_linear_target_residualizer(
+                z,
+                nuisance_arrays,
+                nuisance_resolution,
+                sample_weight=None,
+            )
+            residualized, valid_mask = _apply_linear_target_residualizer(
+                z,
+                nuisance_arrays,
+                residualizer,
+            )
+            fallback_mask = ~valid_mask
+            if np.any(fallback_mask):
+                residualized[fallback_mask] = z[fallback_mask]
+            residualization_status = "linear_residualized"
+        except Exception as exc:
+            residualization_status = f"identity_fallback:{exc}"
+            residualizer = None
+
+    payoff_target = np.maximum(residualized, 0.0).astype(np.float32, copy=False)
+    favorable_return = (side_sign * y_ret_arr).astype(np.float32, copy=False)
+    return {
+        "target": payoff_target,
+        "side_adjusted_return": favorable_return,
+        "raw_vol_norm_return": z.astype(np.float32, copy=False),
+        "residualized_return": residualized.astype(np.float32, copy=False),
+        "vol_source": vol_source,
+        "residualizer": residualizer,
+        "residualization_status": residualization_status,
+        "nuisance_columns": list(nuisance_arrays.keys()),
+        "target_name": "positive_part_residualized_return_over_realized_vol",
+    }
+
+
+def _build_base_regression_sample_weight(
+    y_long: np.ndarray,
+    residualized_return: np.ndarray,
+) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    target = np.asarray(y_long, dtype=np.float32)
+    resid = np.asarray(residualized_return, dtype=np.float32)
+    if target.shape[0] != resid.shape[0]:
+        raise ValueError("Base reg sample weight: target/residual length mismatch")
+    weights = np.ones(target.shape[0], dtype=np.float32)
+    pos = np.isfinite(target) & (target > 0.0)
+    if np.any(pos):
+        cap = float(np.nanpercentile(target[pos], 95.0))
+        if np.isfinite(cap) and cap > 1e-12:
+            alpha = 2.0 / cap
+            weights[pos] = 1.0 + alpha * np.minimum(target[pos], np.float32(cap))
+        else:
+            alpha = 0.0
+    else:
+        cap = float("nan")
+        alpha = 0.0
+
+    neg = np.isfinite(resid) & (resid < 0.0) & (~pos)
+    neg_scale = float("nan")
+    neg_max = 2.0
+    if np.any(neg):
+        neg_abs = np.abs(resid[neg]).astype(np.float32, copy=False)
+        neg_scale = float(np.nanpercentile(neg_abs, 75.0))
+        if np.isfinite(neg_scale) and neg_scale > 1e-12:
+            weights[neg] = 1.0 + (neg_max - 1.0) * (
+                1.0 - np.exp(-neg_abs / np.float32(neg_scale))
+            )
+    weights = np.where(np.isfinite(weights), weights, 1.0).astype(
+        np.float32, copy=False
+    )
+    return {
+        "sample_weight": weights,
+        "cap": float(cap),
+        "alpha": float(alpha),
+        "positive_count": int(np.sum(pos)),
+        "negative_count": int(np.sum(neg)),
+        "neg_scale": float(neg_scale),
+        "neg_max": float(neg_max),
+    }
+

+    def _align_feature_matrix(_name: str, _value):
+        expected_size = int(n_ts * n_syms)
+        if isinstance(_value, pd.DataFrame):
+            aligned = _value.reindex(index=close_df.index, columns=close_df.columns)

+def _robust_sigma_rows(x_2d: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
+    arr = np.asarray(x_2d, dtype=np.float32)
+    if arr.ndim != 2 or arr.size == 0:
+        return np.full(arr.shape[0] if arr.ndim > 0 else 0, np.nan, dtype=np.float32)
+    med = np.nanmedian(arr, axis=1)
+    mad = np.nanmedian(np.abs(arr - med[:, None]), axis=1)
+    return np.asarray(1.4826 * mad, dtype=np.float32)
+
+
+def _safe_nanpercentile_rows(
+    x_2d: np.ndarray, q: float, fallback: float = 0.0
+) -> np.ndarray:
+    arr = np.asarray(x_2d, dtype=np.float32)
+    if arr.ndim != 2 or arr.size == 0:
+        return np.full(arr.shape[0] if arr.ndim > 0 else 0, fallback, dtype=np.float32)
+    try:
+        out = np.nanpercentile(arr, q, axis=1).astype(np.float32)
+    except Exception:
+        out = np.full(arr.shape[0], fallback, dtype=np.float32)
+    return np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, np.float32(fallback)).astype(np.float32)
+
+
+def _binary_entropy_from_fraction(frac: np.ndarray, eps: float = 1e-12) -> np.ndarray:
+    p = np.clip(np.asarray(frac, dtype=np.float32), eps, 1.0 - eps)
+    return np.asarray(
+        -(p * np.log(p) + (1.0 - p) * np.log(1.0 - p)),
+        dtype=np.float32,
+    )
+
+
+def _safe_linear_calibration_summary(
+    y_true: np.ndarray, y_pred: np.ndarray
+) -> tuple[float, float]:
+    y_t = np.asarray(y_true, dtype=np.float64)
+    y_p = np.asarray(y_pred, dtype=np.float64)
+    mask = np.isfinite(y_t) & np.isfinite(y_p)
+    if int(mask.sum()) < 10:
+        return float("nan"), float("nan")
+    x = y_p[mask]
+    y = y_t[mask]
+    x_std = float(np.std(x))
+    if x_std <= 1e-12:
+        return float("nan"), float("nan")
+    try:
+        lr = LinearRegression()
+        lr.fit(x.reshape(-1, 1), y)
+        return float(lr.coef_[0]), float(lr.intercept_)
+    except Exception:
+        return float("nan"), float("nan")
+
+
+def _aggregate_base_reg_oof_metrics(
+    *,
+    y_true: np.ndarray,
+    y_pred: np.ndarray,
+    y_ret: np.ndarray,
+    q10: np.ndarray,
+    q50: np.ndarray,
+    q90: np.ndarray,
+    groups=None,
+) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    yt = np.asarray(y_true, dtype=np.float64)
+    yp = np.asarray(y_pred, dtype=np.float64)
+    yr = np.asarray(y_ret, dtype=np.float64)
+    q10_arr = np.asarray(q10, dtype=np.float64)
+    q50_arr = np.asarray(q50, dtype=np.float64)
+    q90_arr = np.asarray(q90, dtype=np.float64)
+    mask = (
+        np.isfinite(yt)
+        & np.isfinite(yp)
+        & np.isfinite(yr)
+        & np.isfinite(q10_arr)
+        & np.isfinite(q50_arr)
+        & np.isfinite(q90_arr)
+    )
+    if mask.sum() < 25:
+        return {
+            "n": int(mask.sum()),
+            "fit_status": "fallback",
+            "metrics_are_fallback": True,
+            "failure_reason": "insufficient_finite_reg_head_oof",
+        }
+
+    yt = yt[mask]
+    yp = yp[mask]
+    yr = yr[mask]
+    q10_arr = q10_arr[mask]
+    q50_arr = q50_arr[mask]
+    q90_arr = q90_arr[mask]
+    grp = np.asarray(groups)[mask] if groups is not None else None
+
+    def _top_ic(top_frac: float) -> float:
+        if len(yp) < 10:
+            return float("nan")
+        n_top = max(5, int(np.ceil(len(yp) * float(top_frac))))
+        idx = np.argsort(yp)[-n_top:]
+        return float(_safe_spearman(yp[idx], yr[idx]))
+
+    def _bucket_monotonicity_score(n_buckets: int = 10) -> float:
+        if len(yp) < max(50, n_buckets * 5):
+            return float("nan")
+        order = np.argsort(yp)
+        buckets = np.array_split(order, n_buckets)
+        means = np.array(
+            [np.nanmean(yr[idx]) if len(idx) else np.nan for idx in buckets],
+            dtype=np.float64,
+        )
+        valid = np.isfinite(means)
+        if int(valid.sum()) < 3:
+            return float("nan")
+        x = np.arange(len(means), dtype=np.float64)[valid]
+        return float(_safe_spearman(x, means[valid]))
+
+    def _magnitude_alignment() -> float:
+        if len(yp) < 25:
+            return float("nan")
+        return float(_safe_spearman(np.abs(yp), np.abs(yt)))
+
+    def _sign_accuracy_by_prediction_decile(n_buckets: int = 10) -> dict[str, float]:
+        out: dict[str, float] = {}
+        if len(yp) < max(50, n_buckets * 5):
+            return out
+        order = np.argsort(yp)
+        buckets = np.array_split(order, n_buckets)
+        pred_sign = np.sign(yp)
+        true_sign = np.sign(yt)
+        for i, idx in enumerate(buckets, start=1):
+            if len(idx) == 0:
+                continue
+            out[f"sign_acc_decile_{i}"] = float(
+                np.mean(pred_sign[idx] == true_sign[idx])
+            )
+        return out
+
+    def _pinball(yv: np.ndarray, qv: np.ndarray, alpha: float) -> float:
+        err = yv - qv
+        loss = np.maximum(alpha * err, (alpha - 1.0) * err)
+        return float(np.nanmean(loss))
+
+    n_top10 = max(5, int(np.ceil(0.10 * len(yp))))
+    idx_top10 = np.argsort(yp)[-n_top10:]
+    idx_bot10 = np.argsort(yp)[:n_top10]
+    spread10 = float(np.nanmean(yr[idx_top10]) - np.nanmean(yr[idx_bot10]))
+    n_top30 = max(10, int(np.ceil(0.30 * len(yp))))
+    idx_top30 = np.argsort(yp)[-n_top30:]
+    idx_bot30 = np.argsort(yp)[:n_top30]
+    spread30 = float(np.nanmean(yr[idx_top30]) - np.nanmean(yr[idx_bot30]))
+
+    cal_slope, cal_intercept = _safe_linear_calibration_summary(yt, yp)
+    cov_q10 = float(np.mean(yt <= q10_arr))
+    cov_q50 = float(np.mean(yt <= q50_arr))
+    cov_q90 = float(np.mean(yt <= q90_arr))
+    interval_80 = float(np.mean((yt >= q10_arr) & (yt <= q90_arr)))
+    top10_sign_precision = float(
+        np.mean(np.sign(yr[idx_top10]) == np.sign(yp[idx_top10]))
+    )
+    top10_hit_rate = float(np.mean(yr[idx_top10] > 0.0))
+
+    metrics = {
+        "n": int(len(yp)),
+        "target_mean": float(np.mean(yt)),
+        "target_std": float(np.std(yt)),
+        "pred_mean": float(np.mean(yp)),
+        "pred_std": float(np.std(yp)),
+        "pred_mae": float(np.mean(np.abs(yt - yp))),
+        "pred_rmse": float(np.sqrt(np.mean(np.square(yt - yp)))),
+        "ic": float(_safe_spearman(yp, yt)),
+        "ic_ret": float(_safe_spearman(yp, yr)),
+        "rank_corr_pred_target": float(_safe_spearman(yp, yt)),
+        "ic_top10": _top_ic(0.10),
+        "ic_top20": _top_ic(0.20),
+        "ic_top30": _top_ic(0.30),
+        "bucket_monotonicity": _bucket_monotonicity_score(10),
+        "magnitude_alignment": _magnitude_alignment(),
+        "spread10": spread10,
+        "spread30": spread30,
+        "top10_hit_rate": top10_hit_rate,
+        "top10_sign_precision": top10_sign_precision,
+        "pinball_q10": _pinball(yt, q10_arr, 0.10),
+        "pinball_q50": _pinball(yt, q50_arr, 0.50),
+        "pinball_q90": _pinball(yt, q90_arr, 0.90),
+        "coverage_q10": cov_q10,
+        "coverage_q50": cov_q50,
+        "coverage_q90": cov_q90,
+        "interval80_coverage": interval_80,
+        "mean_upside_tail": float(np.mean(q90_arr - q50_arr)),
+        "mean_uncertainty": float(np.mean(q90_arr - q10_arr)),
+        "calibration_slope": cal_slope,
+        "calibration_intercept": cal_intercept,
+        "quantile_crossing_rate": float(
+            np.mean((q10_arr > q50_arr) | (q50_arr > q90_arr))
+        ),
+        "fit_status": "trained",
+        "metrics_are_fallback": False,
+        "failure_reason": None,
+    }
+    metrics.update(_sign_accuracy_by_prediction_decile(10))
+    if grp is not None and len(grp) == len(yp):
+        metrics["ic_cs"] = float(ic_cross_sectional(yp, yr, groups=grp))
+    return metrics
+
+
+def _compute_reg_head_interactions(
+    clf_dm: dict[str, Any], reg_features: dict[str, np.ndarray]
+) -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:
+    n = len(next(iter(reg_features.values()))) if reg_features else 0
+    clf_prob = np.asarray(clf_dm.get("oof_probs"), dtype=np.float32)
+    if clf_prob.ndim != 1 or len(clf_prob) != n:
+        clf_prob = np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=np.float32)
+    clf_entropy = np.asarray(
+        clf_dm.get("oof_tree_vote_entropy", np.full(n, np.nan, dtype=np.float32)),
+        dtype=np.float32,
+    )
+    clf_vote_margin = np.asarray(
+        clf_dm.get("oof_tree_vote_margin", np.full(n, np.nan, dtype=np.float32)),
+        dtype=np.float32,
+    )
+    clf_std = np.asarray(
+        clf_dm.get("oof_tree_pred_std", np.full(n, np.nan, dtype=np.float32)),
+        dtype=np.float32,
+    )
+    reg_q50 = np.asarray(reg_features.get("reg_q50"), dtype=np.float32)
+    reg_snr = np.asarray(reg_features.get("reg_pred_snr"), dtype=np.float32)
+    reg_entropy = np.asarray(reg_features.get("reg_sign_entropy"), dtype=np.float32)
+    reg_std = np.asarray(reg_features.get("reg_pred_std_robust"), dtype=np.float32)
+    clf_center = np.asarray((2.0 * clf_prob) - 1.0, dtype=np.float32)
+    reg_sign = np.sign(reg_q50).astype(np.float32)
+    clf_sign = np.sign(clf_center).astype(np.float32)
+    return {
+        "clf_reg_direction_score": (clf_sign * reg_sign).astype(np.float32),
+        "clf_prob_x_reg_snr": (clf_prob * reg_snr).astype(np.float32),
+        "reg_snr_x_clf_vote_margin": (reg_snr * clf_vote_margin).astype(np.float32),
+        "clf_entropy_x_reg_sign_entropy": (clf_entropy * reg_entropy).astype(
+            np.float32
+        ),
+        "clf_std_x_reg_std": (clf_std * reg_std).astype(np.float32),
+    }
+
+
+def _compute_base_reg_head_oof_features(
+    *,
+    model,
+    X_eval,
+    X_train,
+    y_train,
+    eps: float = 1e-12,
+) -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:
+    inner = model
+    estimators = getattr(inner, "estimators_", None)
+    n = len(X_eval)
+    if not estimators:
+        return {
+            key: np.full(n, np.nan, dtype=np.float32)
+            for key in _BASE_REG_UNCERTAINTY_KEYS
+        }
+
+    race_helper = ModelRace(kind="base_reg_head", task="base_reg", n_splits=2)
+    X_np = np.asarray(X_eval, dtype=np.float32)
+    X_tr = np.asarray(X_train, dtype=np.float32)
+    y_tr = np.asarray(y_train, dtype=np.float32).reshape(-1)
+    try:
+        tree_preds = np.column_stack([t.predict(X_np) for t in estimators]).astype(
+            np.float32
+        )
+    except Exception:
+        return {
+            key: np.full(n, np.nan, dtype=np.float32)
+            for key in _BASE_REG_UNCERTAINTY_KEYS
+        }
+
+    q10 = _safe_nanpercentile_rows(tree_preds, 10.0, fallback=np.nan)
+    q50 = _safe_nanpercentile_rows(tree_preds, 50.0, fallback=np.nan)
+    q90 = _safe_nanpercentile_rows(tree_preds, 90.0, fallback=np.nan)
+    reg_mean = np.nanmean(tree_preds, axis=1).astype(np.float32)
+    reg_std_robust = _robust_sigma_rows(tree_preds)
+    reg_snr = (np.abs(reg_mean) / (reg_std_robust + eps)).astype(np.float32)
+    reg_range = (q90 - q10).astype(np.float32)
+    pos_vote_frac = np.nanmean((tree_preds > 0.0).astype(np.float32), axis=1).astype(
+        np.float32
+    )
+    sign_entropy = _binary_entropy_from_fraction(pos_vote_frac, eps=eps)
+    sign_margin = np.abs((2.0 * pos_vote_frac) - 1.0).astype(np.float32)
+
+    leaf_context = race_helper._build_leaf_context(model, X_tr, y_tr)
+    n_trees = len(estimators)
+    leaf_support = np.full((n, n_trees), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)
+    leaf_target_iqr = np.full((n, n_trees), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)
+    leaf_centroid_dist = np.full((n, n_trees), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)
+    for tree_i, tree in enumerate(estimators):
+        leaves = tree.apply(X_np).astype(np.int32)
+        leaf_support[:, tree_i] = tree.tree_.n_node_samples[leaves].astype(np.float32)
+        if not leaf_context or tree_i >= len(leaf_context):
+            continue
+        ctx = leaf_context[tree_i]
+        leaf_ids = ctx["leaf_ids"]
+        pos = np.searchsorted(leaf_ids, leaves)
+        valid = (pos >= 0) & (pos < len(leaf_ids)) & (leaf_ids[pos] == leaves)
+        if not valid.any():
+            continue
+        vp = pos[valid]
+        leaf_target_iqr[valid, tree_i] = ctx["target_iqr"][vp]
+        centroids = ctx["centroid"][vp]
+        diff = X_np[valid] - centroids
+        leaf_centroid_dist[valid, tree_i] = np.sqrt(
+            np.sum(np.square(diff, dtype=np.float32), axis=1),
+            dtype=np.float32,
+        )
+
+    leaf_support_mean = np.nanmean(leaf_support, axis=1).astype(np.float32)
+    leaf_support_q25 = np.nanpercentile(leaf_support, 25.0, axis=1).astype(np.float32)
+    leaf_target_iqr_mean = np.nanmean(leaf_target_iqr, axis=1).astype(np.float32)
+    leaf_centroid_dist_mean = np.nanmean(leaf_centroid_dist, axis=1).astype(np.float32)
+    leaf_centroid_dist_rel = leaf_centroid_dist / (
+        leaf_centroid_dist_mean[:, np.newaxis] + eps
+    )
+    leaf_centroid_dist_rel_mean = np.nanmean(leaf_centroid_dist_rel, axis=1).astype(
+        np.float32
+    )
+    leaf_centroid_dist_rel_std = np.nanstd(leaf_centroid_dist_rel, axis=1).astype(
+        np.float32
+    )
+
+    out = {
+        "reg_pred_mean": reg_mean,
+        "reg_pred_std_robust": reg_std_robust,
+        "reg_pred_snr": reg_snr,
+        "reg_pred_range_q90_q10": reg_range,
+        "reg_sign_entropy": sign_entropy,
+        "reg_sign_margin": sign_margin,
+        "reg_pos_vote_frac": pos_vote_frac,
+        "reg_leaf_support_mean": leaf_support_mean,
+        "reg_leaf_support_q25": leaf_support_q25,
+        "reg_leaf_target_iqr_mean": leaf_target_iqr_mean,
+        "reg_leaf_centroid_dist_rel_mean": leaf_centroid_dist_rel_mean,
+        "reg_leaf_centroid_dist_rel_std": leaf_centroid_dist_rel_std,
+        "reg_q10": q10.astype(np.float32),
+        "reg_q50": q50.astype(np.float32),
+        "reg_q90": q90.astype(np.float32),
+        "reg_upside_tail": (q90 - q50).astype(np.float32),
+        "reg_uncertainty": (q90 - q10).astype(np.float32),
+    }
+    return {
+        key: np.where(np.isfinite(val), val, np.nan).astype(np.float32)
+        for key, val in out.items()
+    }
+
+
+def _fit_direct_extratrees_reg_head(
+    *,
+    kind_name,
+    hpo_scope_key=None,
+    X,
+    y_reg,
+    y_ret,
+    sample_weight=None,
+    groups=None,
+    n_splits: int = 2,
+    cfg=None,
+    clf_detailed_metrics: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
+):
+    from sklearn.base import clone
+    from sklearn.ensemble import ExtraTreesRegressor
+    from sklearn.isotonic import IsotonicRegression
+
+    from .post_race_hpo import load_best_base_extratrees_payload
+    from .purged_cv import PurgedKFold as _PurgedKFold
+
+    if hasattr(X, "select_dtypes"):
+        X = X.select_dtypes(include=[np.number]).copy()
+    if hasattr(X, "to_numpy"):
+        X_np = X.to_numpy(dtype=np.float32, copy=False)
+    else:
+        X_np = np.asarray(X, dtype=np.float32)
+
+    y_reg_arr = np.asarray(y_reg, dtype=np.float32)
+    y_ret_arr = np.asarray(y_ret, dtype=np.float32)
+    sw_arr = (
+        None if sample_weight is None else np.asarray(sample_weight, dtype=np.float32)
+    )
+    groups_arr = None if groups is None else np.asarray(groups)
+    if X_np.shape[0] != len(y_reg_arr):
+        raise ValueError("Base reg head: X/y length mismatch")
+    if sw_arr is not None and X_np.shape[0] != len(sw_arr):
+        raise ValueError("Base reg head: X/sample_weight length mismatch")
+
+    _hpo_dir = (
+        os.path.join(cfg.get("data_root", "data"), "hpo_out")
+        if cfg is not None
+        else None
+    )
+    if cfg is not None and "base_hpo_out_dir" in cfg:
+        _hpo_dir = cfg["base_hpo_out_dir"]
+    hpo_payload = None
+    if _hpo_dir is not None:
+        _scope = f"{hpo_scope_key or kind_name}__reg_head"
+        hpo_payload = load_best_base_extratrees_payload(_hpo_dir, scope_key=_scope)
+        if hpo_payload is None:
+            hpo_payload = load_best_base_extratrees_payload(
+                _hpo_dir, scope_key=(hpo_scope_key or kind_name)
+            )
+
+    reg_params = {
+        "n_estimators": 400,
+        "max_depth": 8,
+        "min_samples_leaf": 20,
+        "min_samples_split": 40,
+        "max_features": 0.33,
+        "bootstrap": False,
+        "min_impurity_decrease": 1e-5,
+        "ccp_alpha": 1e-6,
+        "max_leaf_nodes": 512,
+        "criterion": "absolute_error",
+        "random_state": 42,
+        "n_jobs": 3,
+    }
+    if hpo_payload is not None and hpo_payload.get("best_params") is not None:
+        best_params = dict(hpo_payload.get("best_params") or {})
+        for key in (
+            "n_estimators",
+            "max_depth",
+            "min_samples_leaf",
+            "min_samples_split",
+            "max_features",
+            "bootstrap",
+            "min_impurity_decrease",
+            "ccp_alpha",
+            "max_leaf_nodes",
+        ):
+            if key in best_params:
+                reg_params[key] = best_params[key]
+        tprint(
+            f"Direct ExtraTrees reg[{hpo_scope_key or kind_name}]: using HPO-derived params "
+            f"max_depth={reg_params['max_depth']}, min_samples_leaf={reg_params['min_samples_leaf']}, "
+            f"min_samples_split={reg_params['min_samples_split']}, max_features={reg_params['max_features']}, "
+            f"n_estimators={reg_params['n_estimators']}, ccp_alpha={reg_params['ccp_alpha']}"
+        )
+    else:
+        tprint(
+            f"Direct ExtraTrees reg[{hpo_scope_key or kind_name}]: using fallback params "
+            "(no base HPO payload found)"
+        )
+
+    model_template = ExtraTreesRegressor(**reg_params)
+    purge_samples = max(2, 10 + 2)
+    embargo_samples = max(2, 10 // 2)
+    tscv = _PurgedKFold(
+        n_splits=n_splits,
+        purge=purge_samples,
+        embargo=embargo_samples,
+        times=groups_arr,
+    )
+    cached_splits = list(tscv.split(X_np))
+    if not cached_splits:
+        tprint(
+            f"Direct ExtraTrees reg[{kind_name}]: skipping reg head because PurgedKFold produced 0 valid folds."
+        )
+        return None
+
+    reg_feature_oof = {
+        key: np.full(len(y_reg_arr), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)
+        for key in _BASE_REG_UNCERTAINTY_KEYS
+    }
+    reg_oof_covered_mask = np.zeros(len(y_reg_arr), dtype=bool)
+    for fold_i, (train_idx, val_idx) in enumerate(cached_splits, start=1):
+        if train_idx.size == 0 or val_idx.size == 0:
+            continue
+        est = clone(model_template)
+        X_tr = X_np[train_idx]
+        X_val = X_np[val_idx]
+        y_tr = y_reg_arr[train_idx]
+        sw_tr = None if sw_arr is None else sw_arr[train_idx]
+        _val_write_idx = np.asarray(val_idx, dtype=np.int32)
+        est.fit(X_tr, y_tr, sample_weight=sw_tr)
+        fold_feats = _compute_base_reg_head_oof_features(
+            model=est,
+            X_eval=X_val,
+            X_train=X_tr,
+            y_train=y_tr,
+        )
+        for key, vals in fold_feats.items():
+            reg_feature_oof[key][_val_write_idx] = np.asarray(vals, dtype=np.float32)
+        reg_oof_covered_mask[_val_write_idx] = True
+        tprint(
+            f"  Direct ExtraTrees reg fold {fold_i}/{len(cached_splits)} complete: n_train={X_tr.shape[0]}, n_val={X_val.shape[0]}"
+        )
+
+    q50 = reg_feature_oof["reg_q50"]
+    expected_reg_oof_coverage = (
+        float(np.mean(reg_oof_covered_mask)) if len(reg_oof_covered_mask) else 0.0
+    )
+    reg_min_finite_fraction = _required_base_oof_finite_fraction(
+        expected_reg_oof_coverage
+    )
+    trainable_ok, trainable_reason = _base_oof_is_trainable(
+        q50,
+        min_finite_fraction=reg_min_finite_fraction,
+        min_unique=2,
+        min_std=1e-12,
+    )
+    if not trainable_ok:
+        tprint(
+            f"WARNING: Direct ExtraTrees reg[{kind_name}] invalid OOF predictions ({trainable_reason}); skipping reg head."
+        )
+        return None
+
+    calibrator = None
+    calibration_method = "identity"
+    raw_q10 = reg_feature_oof["reg_q10"].copy()
+    raw_q50 = reg_feature_oof["reg_q50"].copy()
+    raw_q90 = reg_feature_oof["reg_q90"].copy()
+    finite_cal = (
+        np.isfinite(raw_q10)
+        & np.isfinite(raw_q50)
+        & np.isfinite(raw_q90)
+        & np.isfinite(y_reg_arr)
+    )
+    if int(finite_cal.sum()) >= 100 and np.unique(raw_q50[finite_cal]).size >= 20:
+        try:
+            calibrator = IsotonicRegression(out_of_bounds="clip")
+            cal_q50 = calibrator.fit_transform(
+                raw_q50[finite_cal], y_reg_arr[finite_cal]
+            )
+            shift = (cal_q50 - raw_q50[finite_cal]).astype(np.float32)
+            reg_feature_oof["reg_q50"][finite_cal] = np.asarray(
+                cal_q50, dtype=np.float32
+            )
+            reg_feature_oof["reg_q10"][finite_cal] = (
+                raw_q10[finite_cal] + shift
+            ).astype(np.float32)
+            reg_feature_oof["reg_q90"][finite_cal] = (
+                raw_q90[finite_cal] + shift
+            ).astype(np.float32)
+            calibration_method = "isotonic"
+            tprint(
+                f"Direct ExtraTrees reg[{hpo_scope_key or kind_name}]: isotonic calibration applied to q50 OOF."
+            )
+        except Exception as exc:
+            calibrator = None
+            calibration_method = "identity"
+            tprint(
+                f"Direct ExtraTrees reg[{hpo_scope_key or kind_name}]: calibration fallback to identity: {exc}"
+            )
+    reg_feature_oof["reg_upside_tail"] = (
+        reg_feature_oof["reg_q90"] - reg_feature_oof["reg_q50"]
+    ).astype(np.float32)
+    reg_feature_oof["reg_uncertainty"] = (
+        reg_feature_oof["reg_q90"] - reg_feature_oof["reg_q10"]
+    ).astype(np.float32)
+
+    interaction_oof = _compute_reg_head_interactions(
+        clf_detailed_metrics or {}, reg_feature_oof
+    )
+    reg_feature_oof.update(interaction_oof)
+    reg_valid_mask = (
+        np.isfinite(reg_feature_oof["reg_q10"])
+        & np.isfinite(reg_feature_oof["reg_q50"])
+        & np.isfinite(reg_feature_oof["reg_q90"])
+        & np.isfinite(y_reg_arr)
+        & np.isfinite(y_ret_arr)
+    )
+    reg_metrics = _aggregate_base_reg_oof_metrics(
+        y_true=y_reg_arr[reg_valid_mask],
+        y_pred=reg_feature_oof["reg_q50"][reg_valid_mask],
+        y_ret=y_ret_arr[reg_valid_mask],
+        q10=reg_feature_oof["reg_q10"][reg_valid_mask],
+        q50=reg_feature_oof["reg_q50"][reg_valid_mask],
+        q90=reg_feature_oof["reg_q90"][reg_valid_mask],
+        groups=groups_arr[reg_valid_mask] if groups_arr is not None else None,
+    )
+    reg_metrics["raw_pred_rmse"] = float(
+        np.sqrt(
+            np.nanmean(
+                np.square(
+                    np.asarray(y_reg_arr, dtype=np.float32)
+                    - np.asarray(raw_q50, dtype=np.float32)
+                )
+            )
+        )
+    )
+    reg_metrics["raw_pred_mae"] = float(
+        np.nanmean(
+            np.abs(
+                np.asarray(y_reg_arr, dtype=np.float32)
+                - np.asarray(raw_q50, dtype=np.float32)
+            )
+        )
+    )
+    reg_metrics["calibration_method"] = calibration_method
+    reg_metrics["training_diagnostics"] = {
+        "n_total": int(len(y_reg_arr)),
+        "n_eval": int(np.sum(reg_valid_mask)),
+        "fold_count_requested": int(n_splits),
+        "fold_count_realized": int(len(cached_splits)),
+        "expected_oof_coverage": expected_reg_oof_coverage,
+        "oof_covered_count": int(np.sum(reg_oof_covered_mask)),
+        "target_mean": float(np.nanmean(y_reg_arr)),
+        "target_std": float(np.nanstd(y_reg_arr)),
+        "target_clip_abs": float("nan"),
+        "sample_weight_mean": float(np.nanmean(sw_arr)) if sw_arr is not None else 1.0,
+        "sample_weight_p95": (
+            float(np.nanpercentile(sw_arr, 95.0)) if sw_arr is not None else 1.0
+        ),
+        "mdi_feature_count": int(X_np.shape[1]),
+        "hpo_params_loaded": bool(
+            hpo_payload is not None and hpo_payload.get("best_params") is not None
+        ),
+        "calibration_method": calibration_method,
+    }
+    final_model = clone(model_template)
+    final_model.fit(X_np, y_reg_arr, sample_weight=sw_arr)
+    return {
+        "model": final_model,
+        "feature_cols": list(X.columns) if hasattr(X, "columns") else [],
+        "oof_features": {
+            key: np.asarray(val, dtype=np.float32).copy()
+            for key, val in reg_feature_oof.items()
+        },
+        "metrics": reg_metrics,
+        "target_name": "positive_part_residualized_return_over_realized_vol",
+        "target_clip_abs": float("nan"),
+        "calibrator": calibrator,
+        "calibration_method": calibration_method,
+    }
+
+
 def _avg_trades_per_day_global(scores, k_frac, timestamps):

@@ -1704,6 +2841,76 @@ def _base_oof_degeneracy_summary(
     }


+def _base_oof_is_trainable(
+    probs,
+    *,
+    min_finite_fraction: float = 1.0,
+    min_unique: int = 2,
+    min_std: float = 1e-12,
+) -> tuple[bool, str]:
+    """Return whether an OOF vector is safe to calibrate/refit.
+
+    If a candidate produces non-finite or effectively flat OOF predictions, we
+    skip that model entirely and move to the next candidate instead of trying to
+    calibrate or refit it.
+    """
+    arr = np.asarray(probs, dtype=np.float64)
+    if arr.size == 0:
+        return False, "empty_oof"
+    finite = np.isfinite(arr)
+    finite_count = int(np.sum(finite))
+    if finite_count == 0:
+        return False, "all_oof_nan"
+    if finite_count / max(arr.size, 1) < float(min_finite_fraction):
+        return False, f"oof_nonfinite_fraction:{arr.size - finite_count}/{arr.size}"
+    finite_arr = arr[finite]
+    unique_count = int(len(np.unique(np.round(finite_arr, 8))))
+    if unique_count < int(min_unique):
+        return False, f"oof_unique_lt_{int(min_unique)}"
+    if float(np.std(finite_arr)) < float(min_std):
+        return False, f"oof_std_lt_{float(min_std):g}"
+    return True, "ok"
+
+
+def _required_base_oof_finite_fraction(
+    expected_coverage: float,
+    *,
+    min_floor: float = 0.45,
+    retain_ratio: float = 0.90,
+) -> float:
+    """Coverage-aware finite OOF requirement for walk-forward CV.
+
+    The current PurgedKFold layout reserves an initial train window, so valid
+    OOF coverage is often materially below 100% even when every realized fold
+    predicts cleanly. The trainability gate should therefore require that most
+    of the *expected* covered rows are finite, not that uncovered rows from the
+    initial train window magically become predicted.
+    """
+    cov = float(np.clip(expected_coverage, 0.0, 1.0))
+    if cov <= 0.0:
+        return 1.0
+    return float(min(cov, max(float(min_floor), cov * float(retain_ratio))))
+
+
+def _base_fit_failure_payload(
+    *,
+    failure_reason: str,
+    training_diagnostics: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
+    degeneracy: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
+    extra: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
+) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    payload = {
+        "fit_status": "failed",
+        "failure_reason": str(failure_reason),
+        "metrics_are_fallback": True,
+        "training_diagnostics": dict(training_diagnostics or {}),
+        "degeneracy": dict(degeneracy or {}),
+    }
+    if extra:
+        payload.update(dict(extra))
+    return payload
+
+

+def _write_base_meta_contract_manifest(
+    cfg: dict[str, Any], run_id: str, final_models: dict[str, Any]
+) -> str:
+    artifacts_root = os.path.join(cfg["data_root"], "artifacts", run_id)
+    oof_columns = [
+        "oof_prob",
+        "index",
+        "timestamp",
+        "symbol",
+        "y_bin",
+        "y_ret",
+        "oof_sigma_trees",
+        "oof_sigma_robust",
+        *[f"oof_tree_{k}" for k in ModelRace._TREE_UNCERTAINTY_KEYS],
+        *_BASE_REG_UNCERTAINTY_KEYS,
+        *_BASE_REG_INTERACTION_KEYS,
+    ]
+    strategies_payload = []
+    for side, side_models in (final_models or {}).items():
+        if not isinstance(side_models, dict):
+            continue
+        for strategy_id, conf in side_models.items():
+            models_by_h = (conf or {}).get("models_by_h", {}) or {}
+            horizons = []
+            oof_files = []
+            native_dirs = []
+            for h, h_info in sorted(models_by_h.items()):
+                horizons.append(int(h))
+                oof_files.append(
+                    os.path.join(
+                        artifacts_root, "oof", f"oof_{strategy_id}_H{int(h)}.parquet"
+                    )
+                )
+                native_dirs.append(
+                    os.path.join(
+                        artifacts_root,
+                        "models",
+                        "native",
+                        f"{side}_{strategy_id}_H{int(h)}",
+                    )
+                )
+            strategies_payload.append(
+                {
+                    "trade_side": str(side),
+                    "strategy_id": str(strategy_id),
+                    "horizons": horizons,
+                    "oof_files": oof_files,
+                    "native_model_dirs": native_dirs,
+                }
+            )
+
+    manifest = {
+        "version": 1,
+        "run_id": str(run_id),
+        "primary_only": True,
+        "base_models_intermediate_path": os.path.join(
+            artifacts_root, "base_models_intermediate.pkl"
+        ),
+        "required_model_entry_keys": [
+            "model",
+            "feat_cols",
+            "H",
+            "selected_features",
+            "fit_row_idx",
+            "reg_head",
+        ],
+        "required_reg_head_keys": [
+            "model",
+            "feature_cols",
+            "oof_features",
+            "metrics",
+            "target_name",
+            "target_clip_abs",
+            "calibration_method",
+        ],
+        "required_oof_columns": oof_columns,
+        "strategies": strategies_payload,
+    }
+    path = os.path.join(artifacts_root, "base_meta_contract.json")
+    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+        json.dump(manifest, f, indent=2)
+    tprint(f"Base/meta compatibility contract written to {path}")
+    return path
+

+    def _infer_strategy_from_meta_key(key: str) -> str | None:

+    def _ece_fixed_bin(y_true, y_prob, n_bins: int = 10) -> float:
+        y_t = np.asarray(y_true, dtype=float)
+        y_p = np.asarray(y_prob, dtype=float)
+        mask = np.isfinite(y_t) & np.isfinite(y_p)
+        if int(mask.sum()) < max(20, n_bins):
+            return float("nan")
+        y_t = y_t[mask]
+        y_p = np.clip(y_p[mask], 0.0, 1.0)
+        edges = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_bins + 1)
+        ece = 0.0
+        for b in range(n_bins):
+            lo = float(edges[b])
+            hi = float(edges[b + 1])
+            if b == n_bins - 1:
+                in_bin = (y_p >= lo) & (y_p <= hi)
+            else:
+                in_bin = (y_p >= lo) & (y_p < hi)
+            if not np.any(in_bin):
+                continue
+            pred_mean = float(np.mean(y_p[in_bin]))
+            true_mean = float(np.mean(y_t[in_bin]))
+            ece += abs(pred_mean - true_mean) * float(np.mean(in_bin))
+        return float(ece)
+
+    def _bucket_monotone_count(
+        y_true, score, n_bins: int = 10
+    ) -> tuple[int, list[float]]:
+        y_true = np.asarray(y_true, dtype=float)
+        score = np.asarray(score, dtype=float)
+        mask = np.isfinite(y_true) & np.isfinite(score)
+        y_true = y_true[mask]
+        score = score[mask]
+        if y_true.size < max(20, n_bins):
+            return 0, []
+        # Build equal-count buckets ordered from highest score to lowest score.
+        # The gate only cares about the quality ordering near the top of the ranking,
+        # so preserve that orientation directly instead of relying on quantile edges.
+        order = np.argsort(-score, kind="mergesort")
+        chunks = np.array_split(order, min(int(n_bins), int(y_true.size)))
+        means: list[float] = [
+            float(np.mean(y_true[idx])) if len(idx) else float("nan") for idx in chunks
+        ]
+        top_k = min(4, len(means))
+        if top_k <= 1:
+            return 0, means
+        proper_adjacent = 0
+        violations = 0
+        for i in range(top_k - 1):
+            a = means[i]
+            b = means[i + 1]
+            if not (np.isfinite(a) and np.isfinite(b)):
+                violations += 1
+                continue
+            if a >= b:
+                proper_adjacent += 1
+            else:
+                violations += 1
+        # One exception is acceptable across the top-4 adjacent comparisons.
+        top4_ok = violations <= 1
+        return int(proper_adjacent if top4_ok else proper_adjacent), means
+
+    def _weekly_ir(y_true, score, timestamps) -> tuple[float, float, list[float]]:
+        y_true = np.asarray(y_true, dtype=float)
+        score = np.asarray(score, dtype=float)
+        ts = pd.to_datetime(np.asarray(timestamps), utc=True, errors="coerce")
+        mask = np.isfinite(y_true) & np.isfinite(score) & ~pd.isna(ts)
+        if int(mask.sum()) < 20:
+            return float("nan"), float("nan"), []
+        yt = y_true[mask]
+        sc = score[mask]
+        ts = ts[mask]
+        weeks = pd.Series(ts).dt.to_period("W").astype(str).to_numpy()
+        week_ics: list[float] = []
+        for wk in pd.unique(weeks):
+            m = weeks == wk
+            if int(np.sum(m)) < 10:
+                continue
+            wk_ic = float(_safe_spearman(sc[m], yt[m]))
+            if np.isfinite(wk_ic):
+                week_ics.append(wk_ic)
+        if not week_ics:
+            return float("nan"), float("nan"), []
+        ir_std = float(np.std(week_ics, ddof=1)) if len(week_ics) > 1 else 0.0
+        ir_mean = float(np.mean(week_ics))
+        ir_scale = float(np.sqrt(52.0))
+        if ir_std <= 1e-12:
+            ir = float("inf") if ir_mean > 0.0 else float("nan")
+        else:
+            ir = float((ir_mean / ir_std) * ir_scale)
+        return ir, ir_std, week_ics
+
+    def _top_bottom_spread(y_true, score, frac: float = 0.10) -> float:
+        y_true = np.asarray(y_true, dtype=float)
+        score = np.asarray(score, dtype=float)
+        mask = np.isfinite(y_true) & np.isfinite(score)
+        y_true = y_true[mask]
+        score = score[mask]
+        n = int(y_true.size)
+        if n <= 2:
+            return float("nan")
+        ksel = max(1, int(np.ceil(float(frac) * n)))
+        order = np.argsort(score, kind="mergesort")
+        ib = order[:ksel]
+        it = order[-ksel:]
+        return float(np.mean(y_true[it]) - np.mean(y_true[ib]))
+
+    def _meta_strategy_gate_result(
+        trade_side: str, strategy_id: str
+    ) -> tuple[bool, dict[str, Any]]:
+        reg_key = f"{strategy_id}_reg"
+        clf_key = f"{strategy_id}_clf"
+        aligned_reg_key = f"{trade_side}_{strategy_id}_reg"

+        reg_model = meta_models.get(reg_key_used)
+        clf_model = meta_models.get(clf_key_used)

+            "ic_u_policy": float(cfg.get("meta_strategy_gate_min_ic_u_policy", 0.015)),
+            "spread10_u_policy": float(
+                cfg.get("meta_strategy_gate_min_spread10_u_policy", 0.0)
+            ),
+            "lift10": float(cfg.get("meta_strategy_gate_min_top_decile_lift", 1.15)),
+        }
+        details: dict[str, Any] = {
+            "strategy_id": strategy_id,
+            "trade_side": trade_side,
+            "reg_key": reg_key,
+            "clf_key": clf_key,
+            "reg_key_used": reg_key_used,
+            "clf_key_used": clf_key_used,
+            "thresholds": thresholds,
+        }
+        failures: list[str] = []
+
+        if reg_model is None:
+            failures.append("missing_reg_head")
+        if clf_model is None:

+            _clf_failure_reason = _meta_clf_failures.get(str(strategy_id))

+                f"Meta {strategy_id}: failed meta-quality gate " f"(missing={failures})"
+            )

+
+        reg_target = np.asarray(_bucket_y_ret.get(reg_key_used, []), dtype=float)
+        reg_md = dict(_bucket_metadata.get(reg_key_used, {}) or {})
+        reg_realized = np.asarray(
+            reg_md.get("__y_ret__", reg_md.get("return", np.array([], dtype=float))),
+            dtype=float,
+        )
+        reg_u_policy = np.asarray(
+            reg_md.get(
+                "__u_policy_net__",
+                reg_md.get("u_policy_net", np.array([], dtype=float)),
+            ),
+            dtype=float,
+        )
+        reg_ts = reg_md.get(
+            "__ts__", reg_md.get("timestamp", np.array([], dtype=object))
+        )
+        reg_pred = int(getattr(reg_model, "score_sign", 1)) * np.asarray(
+            getattr(reg_model, "oof_probs", []), dtype=float
+        )
+        n_reg = min(
+            len(reg_pred),
+            len(reg_target),
+            len(reg_realized),
+            len(reg_u_policy),
+            len(reg_ts),
+        )
+        reg_pred = reg_pred[:n_reg]
+        reg_target = reg_target[:n_reg]
+        reg_realized = reg_realized[:n_reg]
+        reg_u_policy = reg_u_policy[:n_reg]
+        reg_ts = np.asarray(reg_ts)[:n_reg]
+        reg_mask_train = (
+            np.isfinite(reg_pred)
+            & np.isfinite(reg_target)
+            & ~pd.isna(pd.to_datetime(reg_ts, utc=True, errors="coerce"))
+        )
+        reg_mask_realized = (
+            np.isfinite(reg_pred)
+            & np.isfinite(reg_realized)
+            & ~pd.isna(pd.to_datetime(reg_ts, utc=True, errors="coerce"))
+        )
+        reg_mask_u_policy = (
+            np.isfinite(reg_pred)
+            & np.isfinite(reg_u_policy)
+            & ~pd.isna(pd.to_datetime(reg_ts, utc=True, errors="coerce"))
+        )
+        reg_ic_train = (
+            float(_safe_spearman(reg_pred[reg_mask_train], reg_target[reg_mask_train]))
+            if int(np.sum(reg_mask_train)) >= 20
+            else float("nan")
+        )
+        reg_ic_return = (
+            float(
+                _safe_spearman(
+                    reg_pred[reg_mask_realized], reg_realized[reg_mask_realized]
+                )
+            )
+            if int(np.sum(reg_mask_realized)) >= 20
+            else float("nan")
+        )
+        reg_ic_u_policy = (
+            float(
+                _safe_spearman(
+                    reg_pred[reg_mask_u_policy], reg_u_policy[reg_mask_u_policy]
+                )
+            )
+            if int(np.sum(reg_mask_u_policy)) >= 20
+            else float("nan")
+        )
+        reg_ir, reg_ic_std, reg_week_ics = _weekly_ir(
+            reg_u_policy[reg_mask_u_policy],
+            reg_pred[reg_mask_u_policy],
+            reg_ts[reg_mask_u_policy],
+        )
+        bucket_mono_count, bucket_means = _bucket_monotone_count(
+            reg_realized[reg_mask_realized], reg_pred[reg_mask_realized], n_bins=10
+        )
+        spread10_return = _top_bottom_spread(
+            reg_realized[reg_mask_realized], reg_pred[reg_mask_realized], frac=0.10
+        )
+        spread10_u_policy = _top_bottom_spread(
+            reg_u_policy[reg_mask_u_policy], reg_pred[reg_mask_u_policy], frac=0.10
+        )
+
+        clf_pred = np.asarray(getattr(clf_model, "oof_probs", []), dtype=float)
+        clf_y = np.asarray(
+            _bucket_y_ret.get(
+                clf_key_used, getattr(clf_model, "y_move", np.array([], dtype=float))
+            ),
+            dtype=float,
+        )
+        n_clf = min(len(clf_pred), len(clf_y))
+        clf_pred = np.clip(clf_pred[:n_clf], 0.0, 1.0)
+        clf_y = np.clip(clf_y[:n_clf], 0.0, 1.0)
+        clf_mask = np.isfinite(clf_pred) & np.isfinite(clf_y)
+        clf_pred = clf_pred[clf_mask]
+        clf_y = clf_y[clf_mask]
+        clf_y_hard = (clf_y >= 0.5).astype(np.int8)
+        base_rate = float(np.mean(clf_y)) if clf_y.size else float("nan")
+        base_rate_hard = float(np.mean(clf_y_hard)) if clf_y_hard.size else float("nan")
+        if clf_pred.size:
+            k10 = max(1, int(np.ceil(0.10 * len(clf_pred))))
+            idx_top10 = np.argsort(-clf_pred)[:k10]
+            p10 = float(np.mean(clf_pred[idx_top10]))
+            r10 = float(np.mean(clf_y[idx_top10]))
+            r10_hard = float(np.mean(clf_y_hard[idx_top10]))
+            top10_lift = (
+                float(r10 / max(base_rate, 1e-12))
+                if np.isfinite(base_rate) and base_rate > 0.0
+                else float("nan")
+            )
+            top10_gap = float(abs(p10 - r10))
+        else:
+            p10 = float("nan")
+            r10 = float("nan")
+            r10_hard = float("nan")
+            top10_lift = float("nan")
+            top10_gap = float("nan")
+        clf_ece = _ece_fixed_bin(clf_y, clf_pred, n_bins=10)
+        clf_brier_soft = (
+            float(np.mean((clf_pred - clf_y) ** 2)) if clf_pred.size else float("nan")
+        )
+        clf_soft_ic = (
+            float(_safe_spearman(clf_pred, clf_y))
+            if clf_pred.size >= 20
+            else float("nan")
+        )
+        clf_auc_hard = float("nan")
+        clf_pr_auc_hard = float("nan")
+        if clf_pred.size >= 20 and len(np.unique(clf_y_hard)) > 1:
+            try:
+                from sklearn.metrics import average_precision_score
+
+                clf_auc_hard = float(roc_auc_score(clf_y_hard, clf_pred))
+                clf_pr_auc_hard = float(average_precision_score(clf_y_hard, clf_pred))
+            except Exception:
+                pass
+
+        checks = {
+            "ic_u_policy": bool(
+                np.isfinite(reg_ic_u_policy)
+                and reg_ic_u_policy >= thresholds["ic_u_policy"]
+            ),
+            "spread10_u_policy": bool(
+                np.isfinite(spread10_u_policy)
+                and spread10_u_policy >= thresholds["spread10_u_policy"]
+            ),
+            "lift10": bool(
+                np.isfinite(top10_lift) and top10_lift >= thresholds["lift10"]
+            ),
+        }
+        passed = bool(all(checks.values()))
+        failed = [k for k, ok in checks.items() if not ok]
+        details.update(
+            {
+                "passed": passed,
+                "failures": failed,
+                "reg_ic": reg_ic_u_policy,
+                "reg_ic_train_target": reg_ic_train,
+                "reg_ic_return": reg_ic_return,
+                "reg_ic_u_policy": reg_ic_u_policy,
+                "reg_ir": reg_ir,
+                "reg_ic_std": reg_ic_std,
+                "reg_week_ic_count": int(len(reg_week_ics)),
+                "reg_spread10_return": spread10_return,
+                "reg_spread10_u_policy": spread10_u_policy,
+                "top10_lift": top10_lift,
+                "bucket_monotone_count_10": int(bucket_mono_count),
+                "bucket_means_10": bucket_means,
+                "ece": clf_ece,
+                "top10_calibration_gap": top10_gap,
+                "top10_pred_mean": p10,
+                "top10_realized_rate": r10,
+                "top10_realized_rate_hard": r10_hard,
+                "clf_base_rate": base_rate,
+                "clf_base_rate_hard": base_rate_hard,
+                "clf_auc_hard": clf_auc_hard,
+                "clf_pr_auc_hard": clf_pr_auc_hard,
+                "clf_brier_soft": clf_brier_soft,
+                "clf_soft_ic": clf_soft_ic,
+                "reg_target_type": "correction_to_base_positive_part_residualized_return_over_realized_vol",
+                "reg_gate_target_type": "u_policy_net_rank",
+                "clf_target_type": "base_correctness_calibration_score",
+                "checks": checks,
+            }
+        )
+        if passed:
+            tprint(
+                f"Meta {strategy_id}: passed meta-quality gate "
+                f"(ic_u_policy={reg_ic_u_policy:.4f}, spread10_u_policy={spread10_u_policy:.6f}, "
+                f"clf_soft_lift10={top10_lift:.4f})"
+            )
+        else:
+            tprint(
+                f"Meta {strategy_id}: blocked by meta-quality gate "
+                f"(failed={failed}, ic_u_policy={reg_ic_u_policy:.4f}, "
+                f"spread10_u_policy={spread10_u_policy:.6f}, clf_soft_lift10={top10_lift:.4f}, mono10={bucket_mono_count}, "
+                f"clf_soft_ic={clf_soft_ic:.4f}, clf_brier_soft={clf_brier_soft:.4f})"
+            )
+        return passed, details
+
+    def _normalize_saved_oof_frame(df_oof: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
+        rename_map = {}
+        if "timestamp" in df_oof.columns and "__ts__" not in df_oof.columns:
+            rename_map["timestamp"] = "__ts__"
+        if "symbol" in df_oof.columns and "__symbol__" not in df_oof.columns:
+            rename_map["symbol"] = "__symbol__"
+        if "y_bin" in df_oof.columns and "__y_bin__" not in df_oof.columns:
+            rename_map["y_bin"] = "__y_bin__"
+        if "y_ret" in df_oof.columns and "__y_ret__" not in df_oof.columns:
+            rename_map["y_ret"] = "__y_ret__"
+        if rename_map:
+            df_oof = df_oof.rename(columns=rename_map)
+        return df_oof
+
+    def _load_saved_oof_frame(
+        trade_side: str, strategy_id: str, horizon: int
+    ) -> pd.DataFrame | None:
+        run_id = str(cfg.get("run_id", "default"))
+        oof_dir = os.path.join(
+            str(cfg.get("data_root", "data")), "artifacts", run_id, "oof"
+        )
+        candidates = [
+            os.path.join(oof_dir, f"oof_{strategy_id}_H{int(horizon)}.parquet"),
+            os.path.join(
+                oof_dir, f"oof_{trade_side}_{strategy_id}_H{int(horizon)}.parquet"
+            ),
+        ]
+        for path in candidates:
+            if not os.path.exists(path):
+                continue
+            try:
+                df_oof = pd.read_parquet(path)
+                if "oof_prob" not in df_oof.columns:
+                    tprint(
+                        f"Meta training: saved OOF artifact missing oof_prob column at {path}"
+                    )
+                    continue
+                tprint(
+                    f"Meta training: loaded saved OOF artifact for {strategy_id} H={int(horizon)} from {path} "
+                    f"(rows={len(df_oof)})"
+                )
+                return _normalize_saved_oof_frame(df_oof)
+            except Exception as e:
+                tprint(f"Meta training: failed to load saved OOF artifact {path}: {e}")
+        return None
+

+    def _collect_primary_tree_uncertainty_features(
+        strategy_id: str,
+        conf_local: dict[str, Any],
+        df_union: pd.DataFrame,
+        horizon_dfs_local: dict[int, tuple[pd.DataFrame, np.ndarray]],
+    ) -> pd.DataFrame:
+        _t_unc_start = _time.monotonic()
+        tprint(
+            f"  Meta {strategy_id}: collecting primary tree uncertainty features "
+            f"for {len(horizon_dfs_local)} horizon(s) over {len(df_union)} union rows..."
+        )
+        out: dict[str, np.ndarray] = {}
+        models_by_h = (conf_local or {}).get("models_by_h", {}) if conf_local else {}
+        for h in sorted(horizon_dfs_local.keys()):
+            _t_h = _time.monotonic()
+            model_info = models_by_h.get(h) or {}
+            race = model_info.get("model")
+            if race is None:
+                tprint(
+                    f"    Meta {strategy_id} H{int(h)}: skipped primary clf uncertainty "
+                    "(missing race model)"
+                )
+                continue
+            ds_h_full = horizon_dfs_local[h][0]
+            fit_row_idx = np.asarray(
+                model_info.get("fit_row_idx", np.arange(len(ds_h_full))), dtype=np.int32
+            )
+            if len(fit_row_idx) > 0:
+                fit_row_idx = fit_row_idx[
+                    (fit_row_idx >= 0) & (fit_row_idx < len(ds_h_full))
+                ]
+            ds_h = (
+                ds_h_full.iloc[fit_row_idx].reset_index(drop=True)
+                if len(fit_row_idx) > 0
+                else ds_h_full.iloc[0:0].copy()
+            )
+            tprint(
+                f"    Meta {strategy_id} H{int(h)}: aligning clf/reg uncertainty on "
+                f"{len(ds_h)} fitted rows -> {len(df_union)} union rows"
+            )
+            for feat_key in _TREE_UNCERTAINTY_KEYS:
+                vec_h = _race_sigma_vector(race, f"oof_tree_{feat_key}", len(ds_h))
+                aligned = _align_values_by_ts_symbol_keys(
+                    df_union["__ts__"].values,
+                    df_union["__symbol__"].values,
+                    ds_h["__ts__"].values,
+                    ds_h["__symbol__"].values,
+                    vec_h,
+                    fill_value=np.nan,
+                    dtype=np.float32,
+                )
+                out[f"pred_{strategy_id}_H{int(h)}_{feat_key}"] = aligned
+                out[f"pred_H{int(h)}_{feat_key}"] = aligned
+                out[f"base_H{int(h)}_{feat_key}"] = aligned
+            reg_head = model_info.get("reg_head") or {}
+            reg_oof_features = dict(reg_head.get("oof_features", {}) or {})
+            for feat_key in _BASE_REG_UNCERTAINTY_KEYS + _BASE_REG_INTERACTION_KEYS:
+                if feat_key not in reg_oof_features:
+                    continue
+                vec_h = np.asarray(reg_oof_features.get(feat_key), dtype=np.float32)
+                if len(vec_h) == 0:
+                    continue
+                aligned = _align_values_by_ts_symbol_keys(
+                    df_union["__ts__"].values,
+                    df_union["__symbol__"].values,
+                    ds_h["__ts__"].values,
+                    ds_h["__symbol__"].values,
+                    vec_h,
+                    fill_value=np.nan,
+                    dtype=np.float32,
+                )
+                out[f"pred_{strategy_id}_H{int(h)}_{feat_key}"] = aligned
+                out[f"pred_H{int(h)}_{feat_key}"] = aligned
+                out[f"base_H{int(h)}_{feat_key}"] = aligned
+            tprint(
+                f"    Meta {strategy_id} H{int(h)}: primary uncertainty alignment complete "
+                f"({_time.monotonic()-_t_h:.1f}s)"
+            )
+        tprint(
+            f"  Meta {strategy_id}: primary tree uncertainty collection complete "
+            f"({_time.monotonic()-_t_unc_start:.1f}s)"
+        )
+        return (
+            pd.DataFrame(out, index=df_union.index)
+            if out
+            else pd.DataFrame(index=df_union.index)
+        )

+            try:

+                from xgboost import XGBClassifier as _XGB3

+                _mcw = float(cfg.get("meta_parallel_forest_min_child_weight", 40.0))

     }
     import json

+    _meta_head_metrics = {}
+    for _mhk, _mhm in meta_models.items():
+        if not hasattr(_mhm, "oof_probs") or _mhm.oof_probs is None:
+            if _mhk.endswith("_cal_reg") and _mhk in _bucket_y_ret:
+                _moof = np.asarray(_bucket_y_ret[_mhk], dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
+            else:
+                continue
+        else:
+            _moof = np.asarray(_mhm.oof_probs, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
+        _mn = len(_moof)
+        _mr = {"n_samples": _mn, "head_type": type(_mhm).__name__}
+        _mr["oof_mean"] = float(np.mean(_moof))
+        _mr["oof_std"] = float(np.std(_moof))
+        _mr["oof_min"] = float(np.min(_moof))
+        _mr["oof_max"] = float(np.max(_moof))
+        _mr["oof_p25"] = float(np.percentile(_moof, 25))
+        _mr["oof_p50"] = float(np.percentile(_moof, 50))
+        _mr["oof_p75"] = float(np.percentile(_moof, 75))
+        _mr["oof_iqr"] = _mr["oof_p75"] - _mr["oof_p25"]
+
+        if isinstance(_mhm, MetaClassifierModel):
+            _my_move = getattr(_mhm, "y_move", None)
+            _my_soft = getattr(_mhm, "y_move_soft", None)
+            if _my_move is not None:
+                _my_move = np.asarray(_my_move, dtype=float).reshape(-1)[:_mn]
+                _mv = np.isfinite(_moof) & np.isfinite(_my_move)
+                if _mv.sum() > 30:
+                    _mr["base_rate"] = float(np.mean(_my_move[_mv]))
+                    try:
+                        from sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score
+
+                        _mr["auc"] = float(roc_auc_score(_my_move[_mv], _moof[_mv]))
+                    except Exception:
+                        pass
+                    _mr["ic"] = float(spearmanr(_moof[_mv], _my_move[_mv]).statistic)
+                    _k10 = max(1, int(0.1 * _mv.sum()))
+                    _top = np.argsort(_moof[_mv])[-_k10:]
+                    _mr["lift_10"] = float(
+                        np.mean(_my_move[_mv][_top])
+                        / max(np.mean(_my_move[_mv]), 1e-12)
+                    )
+                    _bot = np.argsort(_moof[_mv])[:_k10]
+                    _mr["spread_10"] = float(
+                        np.mean(_my_move[_mv][_top]) - np.mean(_my_move[_mv][_bot])
+                    )
+                    from sklearn.metrics import brier_score_loss
+
+                    _mr["brier"] = float(brier_score_loss(_my_move[_mv], _moof[_mv]))
+                    _mr["brier_baseline"] = float(
+                        brier_score_loss(
+                            _my_move[_mv],
+                            np.full(_mv.sum(), np.mean(_my_move[_mv])),
+                        )
+                    )
+                    _mr["brier_improvement"] = _mr["brier_baseline"] - _mr["brier"]
+                    try:
+                        from sklearn.metrics import average_precision_score
+
+                        _mr["pr_auc"] = float(
+                            average_precision_score(_my_move[_mv], _moof[_mv])
+                        )
+                    except Exception:
+                        pass
+                    _top_p = _moof[_mv][_top]
+                    _mr["top10_mean_pred"] = float(np.mean(_top_p))
+                    _mr["top10_actual_rate"] = float(np.mean(_my_move[_mv][_top]))
+            if _my_soft is not None:
+                _my_soft = np.asarray(_my_soft, dtype=float).reshape(-1)[:_mn]
+                _mv2 = np.isfinite(_moof) & np.isfinite(_my_soft)
+                if _mv2.sum() > 30:
+                    _mr["ic_soft"] = float(
+                        spearmanr(_moof[_mv2], _my_soft[_mv2]).statistic
+                    )
+        elif _mhk.endswith("_cal_reg"):
+            _md_bucket = _bucket_metadata.get(_mhk, {})
+            _y_reg_key = _bucket_y_ret.get(_mhk)
+            if _y_reg_key is None:
+                _reg_key = _mhk.replace("_cal_reg", "_reg")
+                if "short_" in _mhk and _reg_key not in _bucket_y_ret:
+                    _reg_key = _mhk.split("_cal_reg")[0].split("short_")[-1] + "_reg"
+                _y_reg_key = _bucket_y_ret.get(_reg_key)
+            if _y_reg_key is not None:
+                _yt = np.asarray(_y_reg_key, dtype=float).reshape(-1)[:_mn]
+                _mv = np.isfinite(_moof) & np.isfinite(_yt)
+                if _mv.sum() > 30:
+                    _mr["target_mean"] = float(np.mean(_yt[_mv]))
+                    _mr["target_std"] = float(np.std(_yt[_mv]))
+                    _mr["ic_target"] = float(spearmanr(_moof[_mv], _yt[_mv]).statistic)
+                    _mr["rmse_target"] = float(
+                        np.sqrt(np.mean((_moof[_mv] - _yt[_mv]) ** 2))
+                    )
+                    _mr["mae_target"] = float(np.mean(np.abs(_moof[_mv] - _yt[_mv])))
+                    _ss_res = np.sum((_moof[_mv] - _yt[_mv]) ** 2)
+                    _ss_tot = np.sum((_yt[_mv] - np.mean(_yt[_mv])) ** 2)
+                    _mr["r2_target"] = float(1.0 - _ss_res / max(_ss_tot, 1e-12))
+            _y_bin_vec = _md_bucket.get("__y_bin__")
+            if _y_bin_vec is not None:
+                _y_bin_arr = np.asarray(_y_bin_vec, dtype=float).reshape(-1)[:_mn]
+                _sig_score = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-np.clip(_moof, -10.0, 10.0)))
+                _mv = np.isfinite(_sig_score) & np.isfinite(_y_bin_arr)
+                _n_valid = int(_mv.sum())
+                if _n_valid > 30:
+                    _y_true = _y_bin_arr[_mv]
+                    _score_prob = _sig_score[_mv]
+                    _score_raw = _moof[_mv]
+                    _base_rate = float(np.mean(_y_true))
+                    _mr["base_rate"] = _base_rate
+                    try:
+                        from sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score
+
+                        _mr["auc"] = float(roc_auc_score(_y_true, _score_prob))
+                        _random_auc = max(_base_rate, 1.0 - _base_rate)
+                        _mr["auc_over_random"] = float(
+                            _mr["auc"] / max(_random_auc, 1e-12)
+                        )
+                    except Exception:
+                        pass
+                    try:
+                        from sklearn.metrics import average_precision_score
+
+                        _mr["pr_auc"] = float(
+                            average_precision_score(_y_true, _score_prob)
+                        )
+                    except Exception:
+                        pass
+                    from sklearn.metrics import brier_score_loss
+
+                    _mr["brier"] = float(brier_score_loss(_y_true, _score_prob))
+                    _mr["brier_baseline"] = float(
+                        brier_score_loss(_y_true, np.full(_n_valid, _base_rate))
+                    )
+                    _mr["brier_improvement"] = _mr["brier_baseline"] - _mr["brier"]
+                    _n_bins = min(10, max(2, _n_valid // 50))
+                    _bin_edges = np.linspace(0, 1, _n_bins + 1)
+                    _bin_idx = np.digitize(_score_prob, _bin_edges[1:-1])
+                    _ece_parts = []
+                    for _bi in range(_n_bins):
+                        _mask_b = _bin_idx == _bi
+                        if _mask_b.sum() == 0:
+                            continue
+                        _conf_b = float(np.mean(_score_prob[_mask_b]))
+                        _acc_b = float(np.mean(_y_true[_mask_b]))
+                        _ece_parts.append(abs(_acc_b - _conf_b) * float(_mask_b.sum()))
+                    _mr["ece"] = float(sum(_ece_parts)) / max(_n_valid, 1)
+                    _k10 = max(1, int(0.10 * _n_valid))
+                    _k20 = max(1, int(0.20 * _n_valid))
+                    _k30 = max(1, int(0.30 * _n_valid))
+                    _top10 = np.argsort(_score_raw)[-_k10:]
+                    _bot10 = np.argsort(_score_raw)[:_k10]
+                    _top20 = np.argsort(_score_raw)[-_k20:]
+                    _top30 = np.argsort(_score_raw)[-_k30:]
+                    _mr["lift_10"] = float(
+                        np.mean(_y_true[_top10]) / max(_base_rate, 1e-12)
+                    )
+                    _mr["lift_20"] = float(
+                        np.mean(_y_true[_top20]) / max(_base_rate, 1e-12)
+                    )
+                    _mr["lift_30"] = float(
+                        np.mean(_y_true[_top30]) / max(_base_rate, 1e-12)
+                    )
+                    _mr["precision_10"] = float(np.mean(_y_true[_top10]))
+                    _mr["precision_20"] = float(np.mean(_y_true[_top20]))
+                    _mr["precision_30"] = float(np.mean(_y_true[_top30]))
+                    _mr["spread_10"] = float(
+                        np.mean(_y_true[_top10]) - np.mean(_y_true[_bot10])
+                    )
+                    _y_pred_hard = (_score_prob >= 0.5).astype(int)
+                    from sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score
+
+                    _mr["accuracy"] = float(accuracy_score(_y_true, _y_pred_hard))
+                    _thr_search = np.linspace(0.1, 0.9, 17)
+                    _best_acc = 0.0
+                    _best_thr = 0.5
+                    for _thr_cand in _thr_search:
+                        _acc_cand = float(
+                            accuracy_score(
+                                _y_true, (_score_prob >= _thr_cand).astype(int)
+                            )
+                        )
+                        if _acc_cand > _best_acc:
+                            _best_acc = _acc_cand
+                            _best_thr = float(_thr_cand)
+                    _mr["best_accuracy"] = _best_acc
+                    _mr["best_threshold"] = _best_thr
+                    _mr["ic_y_bin"] = float(spearmanr(_score_raw, _y_true).statistic)
+            _cal_model = (
+                getattr(_mhm, "model", None) if hasattr(_mhm, "model") else None
+            )
+            if _cal_model is None:
+                _cal_model = _mhm if hasattr(_mhm, "feature_importances_") else None
+            _cal_fi = None
+            if hasattr(_cal_model, "feature_importances_"):
+                _cal_fi = _cal_model.feature_importances_
+            elif hasattr(_cal_model, "get_booster"):
+                try:
+                    _cal_fi = np.asarray(
+                        _cal_model.get_booster()
+                        .get_score(importance_type="gain")
+                        .values(),
+                        dtype=float,
+                    )
+                except Exception:
+                    pass
+            if _cal_fi is not None:
+                _mr["feature_importance_top5_sum"] = float(
+                    np.sum(np.sort(_cal_fi)[-5:]) / max(np.sum(_cal_fi), 1e-12)
+                )
+                _mr["feature_importance_max"] = float(np.max(_cal_fi))
+                _mr["n_features_used"] = int(
+                    np.sum(_cal_fi > 0) if _cal_fi is not None else 0
+                )
+            for _y_col, _metric_name in [
+                ("__u_policy__", "ic_u_policy"),
+                ("__y_ret__", "ic_y_ret"),
+            ]:
+                _yv = _md_bucket.get(_y_col)
+                if _yv is not None:
+                    _yv = np.asarray(_yv, dtype=float).reshape(-1)[:_mn]
+                    _mvy = np.isfinite(_moof) & np.isfinite(_yv)
+                    if _mvy.sum() > 30:
+                        _mr[_metric_name] = float(
+                            spearmanr(_moof[_mvy], _yv[_mvy]).statistic
+                        )
+        else:
+            _my_move = getattr(_mhm, "y_move", None)
+            _y_reg_key = _bucket_y_ret.get(_mhk)
+            if _y_reg_key is not None:
+                _yt = np.asarray(_y_reg_key, dtype=float).reshape(-1)[:_mn]

+    def _record_alpha_fit_diag(
+        side_name,
+        kind_name,
+        horizon,
+        *,
+        fit_status,
+        failure_reason=None,
+        metrics_are_fallback=False,
+        selected_features=None,
+        y_fit=None,
+        training_diag=None,
+        degeneracy=None,
+        extra=None,
+    ):
+        y_arr = None if y_fit is None else np.asarray(y_fit, dtype=np.float32)
+        n_total = (
+            int(len(y_arr))
+            if y_arr is not None
+            else int((training_diag or {}).get("n_total", 0))
+        )
+        if y_arr is not None and len(y_arr) > 0:
+            y_hard = (y_arr >= 0.5).astype(np.int8)
+            n_pos = int(np.sum(y_hard))
+            n_neg = int(len(y_hard) - n_pos)
+        else:
+            class_counts = dict((training_diag or {}).get("class_counts", {}) or {})
+            n_pos = int(class_counts.get("pos", 0))
+            n_neg = int(class_counts.get("neg", 0))
+        minority_pct = float(
+            (min(n_pos, n_neg) / max(n_total, 1)) * 100.0 if n_total > 0 else 0.0
+        )
+        key = f"{side_name}_{kind_name}_H{int(horizon)}"
+        payload = {
+            "fit_status": str(fit_status),
+            "failure_reason": failure_reason,
+            "metrics_are_fallback": bool(metrics_are_fallback),
+            "selected_feature_count": int(len(selected_features or [])),
+            "selected_feature_sample": list(selected_features or [])[:12],
+            "support": {
+                "n_total": n_total,
+                "n_positive": n_pos,
+                "n_negative": n_neg,
+                "minority_support_pct": minority_pct,
+            },
+            "training_diagnostics": dict(training_diag or {}),
+            "degeneracy": dict(degeneracy or {}),
+        }
+        if extra:
+            payload.update(extra)
+        alpha_fit_diagnostics[key] = payload
+        if str(fit_status).lower() in {"failed", "skipped", "fallback"}:
+            _support = payload.get("support", {})
+            _training_diag = payload.get("training_diagnostics", {}) or {}
+            tprint(
+                f"Base fit diagnostic [{key}]: status={payload['fit_status']} "
+                f"reason={payload.get('failure_reason')} n={_support.get('n_total', 0)} "
+                f"pos={_support.get('n_positive', 0)} neg={_support.get('n_negative', 0)} "
+                f"minority_pct={float(_support.get('minority_support_pct', 0.0)):.2f}"
+            )
+            if payload.get("degeneracy"):
+                tprint(f"  Degeneracy detail [{key}]: {payload.get('degeneracy')}")
+            if _training_diag:
+                tprint(f"  Training diagnostics [{key}]: {_training_diag}")
+        return payload

+    def _build_variant_alpha_bundle(side_name, kind_name, horizon):
+        members = []
+        member_oofs = []
+        selected_union = []
+        top5_union = []
+        top10_union = []
+        for variant_name in cfg.get("base_geometry_archetypes", ["tight", "wide"]):
+            variant_name = str(variant_name)
+            variant_info = base_variant_models.get(
+                (side_name, kind_name, int(horizon), variant_name)
+            )
+            if not variant_info:
+                continue
+            race_variant = variant_info.get("model")
+            if race_variant is None or race_variant.oof_probs is None:
+                continue
+            ds_key_variant = f"train_{kind_name}_{int(horizon)}_{variant_name}"
+            df_variant = datasets.get(ds_key_variant)
+            if df_variant is None or df_variant.empty:
+                continue
+            members.append(
+                {
+                    "model": race_variant,
+                    "feat_cols": list(variant_info.get("feat_cols", [])),
+                    "H": int(horizon),
+                    "variant": variant_name,
+                    "oof_probs": np.asarray(race_variant.oof_probs, dtype=np.float32),
+                }
+            )
+            if "__ts__" in df_variant.columns and "__symbol__" in df_variant.columns:
+                aligned_oof = _align_values_by_ts_symbol_keys(
+                    datasets[f"train_{kind_name}_{int(horizon)}"]["__ts__"].values,
+                    datasets[f"train_{kind_name}_{int(horizon)}"]["__symbol__"].values,
+                    df_variant["__ts__"].values,
+                    df_variant["__symbol__"].values,
+                    np.asarray(race_variant.oof_probs, dtype=np.float32),
+                    fill_value=0.5,
+                    dtype=np.float32,
+                )
+            else:
+                n_union = len(datasets[f"train_{kind_name}_{int(horizon)}"])
+                aligned_oof = np.full(n_union, 0.5, dtype=np.float32)
+                n_use = min(n_union, len(race_variant.oof_probs))
+                aligned_oof[:n_use] = np.asarray(
+                    race_variant.oof_probs, dtype=np.float32
+                )[:n_use]
+            member_oofs.append(aligned_oof)
+            selected_union.extend(list(variant_info.get("selected_features", [])))
+            top5_union.extend(list(variant_info.get("top5_features", [])))
+            top10_union.extend(list(variant_info.get("top10_features", [])))
+        if not members:
+            return None
+        ensemble_model = AlphaHorizonEnsemble(members)
+        if member_oofs:
+            ensemble_model.oof_probs = np.mean(np.vstack(member_oofs), axis=0).astype(
+                np.float32
+            )
+        feat_cols_union = list(dict.fromkeys(selected_union))
+        return {
+            "model": ensemble_model,
+            "H": int(horizon),
+            "feat_cols": feat_cols_union,
+            "selected_features": feat_cols_union,
+            "top5_features": list(dict.fromkeys(top5_union))[:5],
+            "top10_features": list(dict.fromkeys(top10_union))[:10],
+            "score": float(np.nan),
+            "econ_ok": True,
+            "degenerate": False,
+            "degeneracy": {},
+            "deployable": True,
+            "variant_members": [m.get("variant") for m in members],
+        }
+
